Nights;}</style><h3 class="css-cs9nlo e17uez1e2">The history of the PR Lions</h3><style data-emotion="css bw6oun">.css-bw6oun{width:100%;margin-top:1.6rem;text-align:left;}@media screen and (max-width: calc(576px - 1px)){.css-bw6oun{margin-top:2.4rem;}}.css-bw6oun p:first-of-type{margin-top:0;}.css-bw6oun p:last-of-type{margin-bottom:0;}@media all and (-ms-high-contrast: none),(-ms-high-contrast: active){.css-bw6oun{-webkit-flex:0 0 auto;-ms-flex:0 0 auto;flex:0 0 auto;}}</style><style data-emotion="css inijr9">.css-inijr9{box-sizing:border-box;margin:0;min-width:0;width:large;height:large;font-weight:normal;font-size:2rem;line-height:2.8rem;width:100%;margin-top:1.6rem;text-align:left;}@media screen and (max-width: calc(576px - 1px)){.css-inijr9{margin-top:2.4rem;}}.css-inijr9 p:first-of-type{margin-top:0;}.css-inijr9 p:last-of-type{margin-bottom:0;}@media all and (-ms-high-contrast: none),(-ms-high-contrast: active){.css-inijr9{-webkit-flex:0 0 auto;-ms-flex:0 0 auto;flex:0 0 auto;}}</style><div class="ezb43302 css-inijr9 e17uez1e3"><div class="css-1btytcd e19rijen0"><p>The PR Lions were introduced in 2009 to celebrate branded communications and creative reputation management.  </p>
<p>It’s been updated and refreshed a number of times over the years to ensure it continues to accurately celebrate and reflect the PR industry.  </p>
<p>In 2019 a comprehensive review of the Lion was undertaken to highlight and celebrate the shift in PR towards a more strategic function. In 2024, Section E ‘Excellence in PR Craft’ was added to provide a dedicated space for independent PR agencies.  </p>
</div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><style data-emotion="css 168pku9">.css-168pku9{box-sizing:border-box;margin:0;min-width:0;-webkit-box-pack:center;-ms-flex-pack:center;-webkit-justify-content:center;justify-content:center;display:-webkit-box;display:-webkit-flex;display:-ms-flexbox;display:flex;gap:4.8rem;}</style><div aria-hidden="true" data-testid="divider-id" spacing="xxlarge" class="css-168pku9 ensbnqn0"><svg width="4" height="4" viewBox="0 0 4 4" fill="none"><circle cx="2" cy="2" r="2" fill="#333333"></circle></svg><svg width="4" height="4" viewBox="0 0 4 4" fill="none"><circle cx="2" cy="2" r="2" fill="#333333"></circle></svg><svg width="4" height="4" viewBox="0 0 4 4" fill="none"><circle cx="2" cy="2" r="2" fill="#333333"></circle></svg></div><div data-testid="spacer-child-wrapper" class="css-1yxbudx e1o176591"><div class="css-vurnku e70vmzt0"><h2 id="pr-guidelines-to-entering" class="e18huyu2 css-13mg45f e17uez1e1">Guidelines to Entering</h2><div class="css-1btytcd e19rijen0"><p><img alt="" src="//images.ctfassets.net/vjs6vfoxg7y6/2q0IyB9tvQ5FXpw83A7WEe/119fb24c650703372e6d8d3615548d80/Line_Gold.webp"></p>
</div><div data-testid="spacer-id" class="css-vurnku e1o176590"><div data-testid="spacer-child-wrapper" class="css-h26fbg e1o176591"><div width="100%" id="pr-what-kind-of-work-can-be-entered" class="ezb43301 css-nc4m1y e70vmzt0"><div class="css-1twr9c3 e70vmzt1"><div height="auto" width="12,,9" class="css-1k50y9j e1958c9o0"><div class="css-1git7hb ezb43300"><h3 class="css-cs9nlo e17uez1e2">What kind of work can be entered?</h3><div class="ezb43302 css-inijr9 e17uez1e3"><div class="css-1btytcd e19rijen0"><p>Campaigns that effectively shape public perception, build meaningful connections with audiences, and drive engagement through storytelling, influence, or advocacy. These can range from press and media campaigns, influencer partnerships, and social media activations to experiential events, crisis communications, and corporate social responsibility initiatives.</p>
</div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div data-testid="spacer-child-wrapper" class="css-h26fbg e1o176591"><div width="100%" id="pr-what-are-the-judging-criteria" class="ezb43301 css-nc4m1y e70vmzt0"><div class="css-1twr9c3 e70vmzt1"><div height="auto" width="12,,9" class="css-1k50y9j e1958c9o0"><div class="css-1git7hb ezb43300"><h3 class="css-cs9nlo e17uez1e2">What are the judging criteria?</h3><div class="ezb43302 css-inijr9 e17uez1e3"><div class="css-1btytcd e19rijen0"><p>A number of criteria will be considered during judging, weighted as follows: 20% idea; 30% PR strategy; 20% PR execution; 30% impact and results.</p>
</div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div data-testid="spacer-child-wrapper" class="css-h26fbg e1o176591"><div width="100%" id="pr-eligibility" class="ezb43301 css-nc4m1y e70vmzt0"><div class="css-1twr9c3 e70vmzt1"><div height="auto" width="12,,9" class="css-1k50y9j e1958c9o0"><div class="css-1git7hb ezb43300"><h3 class="css-cs9nlo e17uez1e2">Eligibility</h3><div class="ezb43302 css-inijr9 e17uez1e3"><div class="css-1btytcd e19rijen0"><p>Work will need to have been executed during the eligibility period of 06 February 2025 - 09 April 2026.</p>
</div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div data-testid="spacer-child-wrapper" class="css-h26fbg e1o176591"><div width="100%" id="pr-is-there-a-limit-to-how-many-times-i-can-enter" class="ezb43301 css-nc4m1y e70vmzt0"><div class="css-1twr9c3 e70vmzt1"><div height="auto" width="12,,9" class="css-1k50y9j e1958c9o0"><div class="css-1git7hb ezb43300"><h3 class="css-cs9nlo e17uez1e2">Is there a limit to how many times I can enter?</h3><div class="ezb43302 css-inijr9 e17uez1e3"><div class="css-1btytcd e19rijen0"><p>The same piece of work can be entered up to four times in PR. </p>
<p><strong>Exceptions:</strong></p>
<ul>
<li>The same piece of work may only be entered once in ‘A. PR: Sectors’. </li>
<li>The work in Section E: Excellence in PR Craft can only be submitted and paid for by independent PR agencies or companies owned by a PR network or independent PR network. The same piece of work can be entered only once in each category within this section.</li>
</ul>
</div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div data-testid="spacer-child-wrapper" class="css-h26fbg e1o176591"><div width="100%" id="awards-what-do-i-need-to-provide-with-my-entry" class="ezb43301 css-nc4m1y e70vmzt0"><div class="css-1twr9c3 e70vmzt1"><div height="auto" width="12,,9" class="css-1k50y9j e1958c9o0"><div class="css-1git7hb ezb43300"><h3 class="css-cs9nlo e17uez1e2">What do I need to provide with my entry?</h3><div class="ezb43302 css-inijr9 e17uez1e3"><div class="css-1btytcd e19rijen0"><p>To enter, you’ll need to complete an online entry form, which can be found on the <a href="https://awards.canneslions.com/index.cfm/entry">Cannes Lions entry site</a>. In the ‘Written Explanation’ section, you’ll be asked to provide the Jury with everything they need to know about your work.</p>
<p>You'll be required provide proof of impact, supported by verifiable evidence, this information will not be shared with the Jury, but will be used to verify claims made in your entry materials. You'll also be required to provide details of a senior executive from both the brand and the entrant company (CEO, CMO or equivalent) who can formally approve the submission. </p>
<p>Optional supporting materials to provide additional context with your entry can also be uploaded. </p>
<p><strong>Detailed information outlining the entry process can be found on our <a href="https://www.canneslions.com/awards/awards-support/awards-entry-guide">Guide to the Cannes Lions Awards</a>.</strong></p>
<p><strong>More information on the rules for entry can be found on the <a href="https://www.canneslions.com/awards/awards-support/rules-and-eligibility">Summary of Key Rules for Entering</a> and the handbook for our new Integrity Standards can be found on the <a href="https://www.canneslions.com/awards/awards-support/integrity-standards">Integrity Standards</a>.</strong></p>
</div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div data-testid="spacer-child-wrapper" class="css-h26fbg e1o176591"><div width="100%" id="awards-what-if-i-dont-have-all-the-details" class="e3k74ef0 css-nc4m1y e70vmzt0"><style data-emotion="css 18q9kjk">.css-18q9kjk{box-sizing:border-box;margin:0;min-width:0;margin-left:-1.2rem;margin-right:-1.2rem;-webkit-box-flex-wrap:wrap;-webkit-flex-wrap:wrap;-ms-flex-wrap:wrap;flex-wrap:wrap;-webkit-box-pack:left;-ms-flex-pack:left;-webkit-justify-content:left;justify-content:left;display:-webkit-box;display:-webkit-flex;display:-ms-flexbox;display:flex;}</style><div class="css-18q9kjk e70vmzt1"><div height="auto" width="12,,9" class="css-1k50y9j e1958c9o0"><style data-emotion="css 1cww4ct">.css-1cww4ct{box-sizing:border-box;margin:0;min-width:0;margin:0 0;max-width:718px;border-left:0.4rem solid #F9BD59;padding:1.6rem 3.2rem;}@media screen and (max-width: calc(576px - 1px)){.css-1cww4ct{padding:0 2.4rem;}}.css-1cww4ct *>p:first-of-type{margin-top:0!important;}.css-1cww4ct *>p:last-of-type{margin-bottom:0!important;}</style><div data-testid="content" class="css-1cww4ct e3k74ef1"><style data-emotion="css ee2cit">.css-ee2cit{text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:bold;}</style><style data-emotion="css 1a1381p">.css-1a1381p{box-sizing:border-box;margin:0;min-width:0;width:xxsmall;height:xxsmall;margin-bottom:1.6rem;font-size:1.4rem;line-height:2.2rem;text-transform:none;font-weight:400;font-family:Riviera Nights;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:bold;}</style><h3 class="e3k74ef2 css-1a1381p e17uez1e2">What if I don’t have all the details right away?</h3><div class="css-1btytcd e19rijen0"><p>Don’t worry – you can fill in the form in any order and save your progress at any time while it’s still in the pending stage. However, please only check out when your entry is 100% complete as no changes can be made to your entry once you’ve clicked  “Submit” and “Checkout”.</p>
</div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div aria-hidden="true" data-testid="divider-id" spacing="xxlarge" class="css-168pku9 ensbnqn0"><svg width="4" height="4" viewBox="0 0 4 4" fill="none"><circle cx="2" cy="2" r="2" fill="#333333"></circle></svg><svg width="4" height="4" viewBox="0 0 4 4" fill="none"><circle cx="2" cy="2" r="2" fill="#333333"></circle></svg><svg width="4" height="4" viewBox="0 0 4 4" fill="none"><circle cx="2" cy="2" r="2" fill="#333333"></circle></svg></div><div data-testid="spacer-child-wrapper" class="css-1yxbudx e1o176591"><div class="css-vurnku e70vmzt0"><h2 id="pr-entry-tips" class="e18huyu2 css-13mg45f e17uez1e1">Entry Tips</h2><div class="css-1btytcd e19rijen0"><p><img alt="" src="//images.ctfassets.net/vjs6vfoxg7y6/2q0IyB9tvQ5FXpw83A7WEe/119fb24c650703372e6d8d3615548d80/Line_Gold.webp"></p>
</div><div data-testid="spacer-id" class="css-vurnku e1o176590"><div data-testid="spacer-child-wrapper" class="css-h26fbg e1o176591"><div width="100%" id="pr-whats-the-jury-looking-for" class="ezb43301 css-nc4m1y e70vmzt0"><div class="css-1twr9c3 e70vmzt1"><div height="auto" width="12,,9" class="css-1k50y9j e1958c9o0"><div class="css-1git7hb ezb43300"><h3 class="css-cs9nlo e17uez1e2">What’s the Jury looking for?</h3><div class="ezb43302 css-inijr9 e17uez1e3"><div class="css-1btytcd e19rijen0"><p>Strategy rooted in earned media that has real impact, beyond likes and impressions. Work that has storytelling at its core and establishes, protects or enhances the reputation and business of an organisation or brand.  </p>
<ul>
<li>A strong insight, impressive execution and real results that link back to the objective </li>
<li>Considered strategy, thoughtfully designed at every touchpoint</li>
</ul>
</div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div data-testid="spacer-child-wrapper" class="css-h26fbg e1o176591"><div width="100%" id="tips-from-the-jury" class="ezb43301 css-nc4m1y e70vmzt0"><div class="css-1twr9c3 e70vmzt1"><div height="auto" width="12,,9" class="css-1k50y9j e1958c9o0"><div class="css-1git7hb ezb43300"><h3 class="css-cs9nlo e17uez1e2">Tips from the Jury</h3><div class="ezb43302 css-inijr9 e17uez1e3"><div class="css-1btytcd e19rijen0"><ul>
<li>Determine the most impressive part of your campaign and find a category that showcases it  </li>
<li>Provide tangible results that demonstrate impact </li>
<li>Remember the Jury are from all over the world, so provide context throughout your entry to ensure they understand the significance of your results and the relevance of your insight </li>
<li>The Jury look at hundreds of pieces of work, use storytelling in the written submission and case film to help your work stand out</li>
</ul>
</div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div data-testid="spacer-child-wrapper" class="css-h26fbg e1o176591"><div width="100%" id="pr-itjr" class="ezb43301 css-nc4m1y e70vmzt0"><div class="css-1twr9c3 e70vmzt1"><div height="auto" width="12,,9" class="css-1k50y9j e1958c9o0"><div class="css-1git7hb ezb43300"><style data-emotion="css 1c9noep">.css-1c9noep{width:100%;margin-top:0;text-align:left;}@media screen and (max-width: calc(576px - 1px)){.css-1c9noep{margin-top:0;}}.css-1c9noep p:first-of-type{margin-top:0;}.css-1c9noep p:last-of-type{margin-bottom:0;}@media all and (-ms-high-contrast: none),(-ms-high-contrast: active){.css-1c9noep{-webkit-flex:0 0 auto;-ms-flex:0 0 auto;flex:0 0 auto;}}</style><style data-emotion="css 1h3p0dj">.css-1h3p0dj{box-sizing:border-box;margin:0;min-width:0;width:large;height:large;font-weight:normal;font-size:2rem;line-height:2.8rem;width:100%;margin-top:0;text-align:left;}@media screen and (max-width: calc(576px - 1px)){.css-1h3p0dj{margin-top:0;}}.css-1h3p0dj p:first-of-type{margin-top:0;}.css-1h3p0dj p:last-of-type{margin-bottom:0;}@media all and (-ms-high-contrast: none),(-ms-high-contrast: active){.css-1h3p0dj{-webkit-flex:0 0 auto;-ms-flex:0 0 auto;flex:0 0 auto;}}</style><div class="ezb43302 css-1h3p0dj e17uez1e3"><div class="css-1btytcd e19rijen0"><p>To hear more from the Jury and how they decided on the winning work, check out the video below.</p>

<h3 id="heres-the-questions-youll-be-asked-regarding-your-work">Here’s the questions you’ll be asked regarding your work.</h3>
<h3 id="to-start-your-entry-to-the-pr-lions-go-to-our-awards-page">To start your entry to the PR Lions, go to our <a href="https://awards.canneslions.com/">Awards page</a>.</h3>
<p>The Jury will refer to the written explanation you provide throughout the judging process. Our advice is to provide as much relevant detail as possible.</p>
<p>When answering the following questions keep the main element you’re trying to celebrate in mind (e.g. the storytelling, the strategy, the craft, the use of data).</p>
<p><em>The bullet points under each question are prompts designed to guide your thinking, based on the factors the jury will be considering when reviewing your work. You might not need to use them all, and that’s totally fine. They’re there to help you consider what’s most important in your work.</em></p>
</div><div data-testid="spacer-id" class="css-vurnku e1o176590"><div data-testid="spacer-child-wrapper" class="css-h26fbg e1o176591"><div width="100%" id="questions" class="ezb43301 css-nc4m1y e70vmzt0"><div class="css-1twr9c3 e70vmzt1"><div height="auto" width="12,,9" class="css-1k50y9j e1958c9o0"><div class="css-1git7hb ezb43300"><h3 class="css-cs9nlo e17uez1e2">Questions</h3></div></div></div></div></div><div data-testid="spacer-child-wrapper" class="css-h26fbg e1o176591"><div width="100%" id="questions" class="e1bpodmc0 css-p7u5up e70vmzt0"><div class="css-yh5xo0 e70vmzt1"><div height="auto" width="12" class="css-1q8vesw e1958c9o0"><div class="css-1mfqvmn e1bpodmc1"></div><div role="tablist" aria-label="" aria-multiselectable="false" class="css-lrnh68 e1bpodmc2"><div class="css-10ryrg8 e1gpwwq90"><div role="tab" aria-controls="accordion-content-0" aria-expanded="false" aria-label="1. Brand Context</p><div class="css-1eruf6x e70vmzt2"><span height="3.5rem" width="3.5rem" data-testid="icon" aria-hidden="true" class="e1gpwwq92 css-nlm7jf e1o6po0j0" display="inline-block"><svg width="100%" height="100%" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#2E2E2E" stroke-width="1px" stroke-linecap="butt" stroke-linejoin="bevel" aria-hidden="true"><g transform="rotate(0 12 12)"><path d="M6 9l6 6 6-6"></path></g></svg></span></div></div><div class="css-1rau9pb e70vmzt1"><div height="auto" role="region" aria-labelledby="accordion-1" id="accordion-content-1" width="12,,9" aria-hidden="true" class="e1gpwwq93 css-19bbyyp e1958c9o0"><div class="css-1m2ur50 e1gpwwq94"><div class="css-1c4mc1w e19rijen0"><p>Please provide any brand and cultural context that would help the jury understand any cultural, national or regional nuances applicable to this work.</p>
<p>You should outline why this piece of work was created for this particular brand, in this market, at this particular moment in time, with all of the cultural and market conditions.</p>
<p><em>The word limit for this section is 250 words.</em></p>
<p><strong>This question is compulsory.</strong></p>
</div></div></div></div></div><div class="css-10ryrg8 e1gpwwq90"><div role="tab" aria-controls="accordion-content-2" aria-expanded="false" aria-label="3. Objective(s)" id="accordion-2" tabindex="0" class="css-1o6m2rl e1gpwwq91"><p color="initial" class="css-1rlptgi e17uez1e3">3. Objective(s)</p><div class="css-1eruf6x e70vmzt2"><span height="3.5rem" width="3.5rem" data-testid="icon" aria-hidden="true" class="e1gpwwq92 css-nlm7jf e1o6po0j0" display="inline-block"><svg width="100%" height="100%" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#2E2E2E" stroke-width="1px" stroke-linecap="butt" stroke-linejoin="bevel" aria-hidden="true"><g transform="rotate(0 12 12)"><path d="M6 9l6 6 6-6"></path></g></svg></span></div></div><div class="css-1rau9pb e70vmzt1"><div height="auto" role="region" aria-labelledby="accordion-2" id="accordion-content-2" width="12,,9" aria-hidden="true" class="e1gpwwq93 css-19bbyyp e1958c9o0"><div class="css-1m2ur50 e1gpwwq94"><div class="css-1c4mc1w e19rijen0"><p>Please select the objective(s) that were most important when creating this work. What the work set out to achieve.</p>
<p>For the jury to accurately assess the work, they must first establish its objectives. As you progress through the entry system, you must link your results back to each objective selected.</p>
<ul>
<li>Overachieve on brand metrics (e.g. awareness, engagement)</li>
<li>Change consumer behaviour (e.g. attitudes, frequency, penetration)</li>
<li>Create short-term sales growth (e.g. sales, promotions, discounts)</li>
<li>Improve brand health (e.g. consideration, preference)</li>
<li>Create sustained sales success (e.g. profit, sales)</li>
<li>Create long-term brand and sales growth (e.g. shareholder value, retention, loyalty)</li>
</ul>
<p><em>There is no word limit for this section.</em></p>
<p><strong>This question is compulsory.</strong></p>
</div></div></div></div></div><div class="css-10ryrg8 e1gpwwq90"><div role="tab" aria-controls="accordion-content-3" aria-expanded="false" aria-label="4. Summary of the work" id="accordion-3" tabindex="0" class="css-1o6m2rl e1gpwwq91"><p color="initial" class="css-1rlptgi e17uez1e3">4. Summary of the work</p><div class="css-1eruf6x e70vmzt2"><span height="3.5rem" width="3.5rem" data-testid="icon" aria-hidden="true" class="e1gpwwq92 css-nlm7jf e1o6po0j0" display="inline-block"><svg width="100%" height="100%" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#2E2E2E" stroke-width="1px" stroke-linecap="butt" stroke-linejoin="bevel" aria-hidden="true"><g transform="rotate(0 12 12)"><path d="M6 9l6 6 6-6"></path></g></svg></span></div></div><div class="css-1rau9pb e70vmzt1"><div height="auto" role="region" aria-labelledby="accordion-3" id="accordion-content-3" width="12,,9" aria-hidden="true" class="e1gpwwq93 css-19bbyyp e1958c9o0"><div class="css-1m2ur50 e1gpwwq94"><div class="css-1c4mc1w e19rijen0"><p>Please provide a summary of your entry.</p>
<ul>
<li>Describe the background and context behind the work?</li>
<li>What creative challenges did the work face?</li>
</ul>
<p>This should be an overview of the work and an analysis of its effectiveness. You should describe how the objectives of the campaign have been successfully met.</p>
<p><em>The word limit for this section is 150 words.</em></p>
<p><strong>This question is compulsory.</strong></p>

Describe the creative idea (20% of vote)" id="accordion-4" tabindex="0" class="css-1o6m2rl e1gpwwq91"><p color="initial" class="css-1rlptgi e17uez1e3">5. Describe the creative idea (20% of vote)</p><div class="css-1eruf6x e70vmzt2"><span height="3.5rem" width="3.5rem" data-testid="icon" aria-hidden="true" class="e1gpwwq92 css-nlm7jf e1o6po0j0" display="inline-block"><svg width="100%" height="100%" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#2E2E2E" stroke-width="1px" stroke-linecap="butt" stroke-linejoin="bevel" aria-hidden="true"><g transform="rotate(0 12 12)"><path d="M6 9l6 6 6-6"></path></g></svg></span></div></div><div class="css-1rau9pb e70vmzt1"><div height="auto" role="region" aria-labelledby="accordion-4" id="accordion-content-4" width="12,,9" aria-hidden="true" class="e1gpwwq93 css-19bbyyp e1958c9o0"><div class="css-1m2ur50 e1gpwwq94"><div class="css-1c4mc1w e19rijen0"><p>Consider the following when describing the creative idea:</p>
<ul>
<li>Does the work cross boundaries/have the ability to think beyond conventional boundaries or categories?</li>
<li>Does the work evoke surprise/have the power to continue captivating attention?</li>
<li>Does the work transcend the original challenge?</li>

Consider:</p>
<ul>

" id="accordion-8" tabindex="0" class="css-1o6m2rl e1gpwwq91"><p color="initial" class="css-1rlptgi e17uez1e3">Was Artificial Intelligence (AI) used in the work or the entry materials? </p><div class="css-1eruf6x e70vmzt2"><span height="3.5rem" width="3.5rem" data-testid="icon" aria-hidden="true" class="e1gpwwq92 css-nlm7jf e1o6po0j0" display="inline-block"><svg width="100%" height="100%" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#2E2E2E" stroke-width="1px" stroke-linecap="butt" stroke-linejoin="bevel" aria-hidden="true"><g transform="rotate(0 12 12)"><path d="M6 9l6 6 6-6"></path></g></svg></span></div></div><div class="css-1rau9pb e70vmzt1"><div height="auto" role="region" aria-labelledby="accordion-8" id="accordion-content-8" width="12,,9" aria-hidden="true" class="e1gpwwq93 css-19bbyyp e1958c9o0"><div class="css-1m2ur50 e1gpwwq94"><div class="css-1c4mc1w e19rijen0"><p>If yes, you must disclose how it was used, considering the following:</p>
<ul>
<li>What AI tools were used?</li>
<li>What was the extent of AI use in the final work?</li>
<li>What were the data sources or inputs for any AI-generated elements, and where did they originate?</li>
</ul>
<p>For guidance on acceptable AI usage please refer to the <a href="https://assets.ctfassets.net/vjs6vfoxg7y6/6n1777aqrVS3u2UbbG0XC6/9207dc5d36fcdf2a8d5061560a8919bd/LIONS_Integrity_Handbook_12.11.2025.pdf">LIONS Integrity Handbook</a>.
Misrepresentation or wilful omission may result in your entry being withdrawn. </p>
<p><em>The word limit for this section is 150 words.</em></p>

</div></div></div></div></div><div class="css-10ryrg8 e1gpwwq90"><div role="tab" aria-controls="accordion-content-9" aria-expanded="false" aria-label="Sustainability Context" id="accordion-9" tabindex="0" class="css-1o6m2rl e1gpwwq91"><p color="initial" class="css-1rlptgi e17uez1e3">Sustainability Context</p><div class="css-1eruf6x e70vmzt2"><span height="3.5rem" width="3.5rem" data-testid="icon" aria-hidden="true" class="e1gpwwq92 css-nlm7jf e1o6po0j0" display="inline-block"><svg width="100%" height="100%" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#2E2E2E" stroke-width="1px" stroke-linecap="butt" stroke-linejoin="bevel" aria-hidden="true"><g transform="rotate(0 12 12)"><path d="M6 9l6 6 6-6"></path></g></svg></span></div></div><div class="css-1rau9pb e70vmzt1"><div height="auto" role="region" aria-labelledby="accordion-9" id="accordion-content-9" width="12,,9" aria-hidden="true" class="e1gpwwq93 css-19bbyyp e1958c9o0"><div class="css-1m2ur50 e1gpwwq94"><div class="css-1c4mc1w e19rijen0"><p>As a member of AdNet Zero, we believe in the collective responsibility to reduce the carbon impact of developing, producing and running advertising to achieve real net zero. 
Please outline how this piece of work aligns with the brand's sustainability strategy and consider the following: </p>
<ul>
<li>Environmental cost: What steps were taken to measure, reduce and manage the environmental footprint of the work across its life cycle? </li>
<li>Meaningful claims: What evidence can you provide to support any sustainability related claims made in the work, ensuring they are credible and meaningful? </li>
<li>Long-term commitments: How do the sustainability-related claims reflect the brand's broader strategy and focus on relevant issues?</li>
</ul>
<p>Please provide details of the benchmarks used to measure the success of this work's contribution to the overall strategy. This could include but is not limited to targets related to advertising business operations - advertising production - media planning and buying. </p>
<p><em>There is no word limit for this section.</em></p>
<p><strong>This question is optional.</strong></p>

Breakthrough on a Budget</p><div class="css-1eruf6x e70vmzt2"><span height="3.5rem" width="3.5rem" data-testid="icon" aria-hidden="true" class="e1gpwwq92 css-nlm7jf e1o6po0j0" display="inline-block"><svg width="100%" height="100%" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#2E2E2E" stroke-width="1px" stroke-linecap="butt" stroke-linejoin="bevel" aria-hidden="true"><g transform="rotate(0 12 12)"><path d="M6 9l6 6 6-6"></path></g></svg></span></div></div><div class="css-1rau9pb e70vmzt1"><div height="auto" role="region" aria-labelledby="accordion-7" id="accordion-content-7" width="12,,9" aria-hidden="true" class="e1gpwwq93 css-19bbyyp e1958c9o0"><div class="css-1m2ur50 e1gpwwq94"><div class="css-1c4mc1w e19rijen0"><p>Budget Details </p>
<p>Please provide budget details that show how the campaign achieved breakthrough results on a limited budget. Consider:</p>
<ul>
<li>the overall budget</li>
<li>the breakdown of costs</li>
<li>how the campaign maximised reach vs expenditure</li>
<li>the significant impact this adjustment had on the campaign's effectiveness.</li>
</ul>
<p>We recommend providing as much context as possible to understand the budget spend. However, we understand that different cases will have different types of results.</p>
<p>*(Any confidential information should be entered in the confidential information field)</p>
<p><em>The word limit for this section is 150 words.</em></p>
<p><strong>This question is compulsory for F06.</strong></p>

css-hwtnfc e19rijen0"><p>As Awards Experts, we know the Lions inside out. We can help with:</p>
<ul>
<li>Sharing  the updates to the Awards for 2026</li>
<li>Offering best-practice advice on how to craft a compelling  entry </li>
<li>Discussing your work and relevant categories </li>
<li>Scheduling regular catch-ups to answer any questions that arise throughout the entry process.</li>
</ul>
<p>Whether you have a specific query or would like expert advice, get in touch today.</p>
<p>Email: <a href="mailto:awards@canneslions.com">awards@canneslions.com</a>
Helpline: +44 0 20 7046 1155</p>

hero.png","contentType":"image/png"}}}}},"prefooterBanner":null,"content":[{"metadata":{"tags":[],"concepts":[]},"sys":{"space":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"Space","id":"vjs6vfoxg7y6"}},"id":"5kybFMOTWlNWsYsZ1T7Mfz","type":"Entry","createdAt":"2024-12-03T13:33:44.246Z","updatedAt":"2026-01-05T16:17:14.411Z","environment":{"sys":{"id":"master","type":"Link","linkType":"Environment"}},"publishedVersion":14,"revision":4,"contentType":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"ContentType","id":"longFormSection"}},"locale":"en-US"},"fields":{"instanceName":"PR / Introduction","title":"Introduction","copy":"![ ](//images.ctfassets.net/vjs6vfoxg7y6/2q0IyB9tvQ5FXpw83A7WEe/119fb24c650703372e6d8d3615548d80/Line_Gold.webp)\n","content":[{"metadata":{"tags":[],"concepts":[]},"sys":{"space":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"Space","id":"vjs6vfoxg7y6"}},"id":"2fB4de5bAr5eHyTVWyAixH","type":"Entry","createdAt":"2024-12-03T12:49:52.926Z","updatedAt":"2026-01-05T16:16:35.758Z","environment":{"sys":{"id":"master","type":"Link","linkType":"Environment"}},"publishedVersion":6,"revision":3,"contentType":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"ContentType","id":"sectionHeader"}},"locale":"en-US"},"fields":{"instanceName":"The history of the PR Lions","title":"The history of the PR Lions","copy":"The PR Lions were introduced in 2009 to celebrate branded communications and creative reputation management.  \n\nIt’s been updated and refreshed a number of times over the years to ensure it continues to accurately celebrate and reflect the PR industry.  \n\nIn 2019 a comprehensive review of the Lion was undertaken to highlight and celebrate the shift in PR towards a more strategic function. In 2024, Section E ‘Excellence in PR Craft’ was added to provide a dedicated space for independent PR agencies.  "}}]}},{"metadata":{"tags":[],"concepts":[]},"sys":{"space":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"Space","id":"vjs6vfoxg7y6"}},"id":"EpVbiEBaG0VtbqJyycdN3","type":"Entry","createdAt":"2024-12-03T15:36:57.127Z","updatedAt":"2026-01-05T16:21:05.098Z","environment":{"sys":{"id":"master","type":"Link","linkType":"Environment"}},"publishedVersion":14,"revision":4,"contentType":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"ContentType","id":"longFormSection"}},"locale":"en-US"},"fields":{"instanceName":"PR / Guidelines to Entering","title":"Guidelines to Entering","copy":"![ ](//images.ctfassets.net/vjs6vfoxg7y6/2q0IyB9tvQ5FXpw83A7WEe/119fb24c650703372e6d8d3615548d80/Line_Gold.webp)","content":[{"metadata":{"tags":[],"concepts":[]},"sys":{"space":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"Space","id":"vjs6vfoxg7y6"}},"id":"M4Fos1WMf6wV2ki4qGbkS","type":"Entry","createdAt":"2024-12-03T13:32:39.196Z","updatedAt":"2026-01-05T16:16:49.300Z","environment":{"sys":{"id":"master","type":"Link","linkType":"Environment"}},"publishedVersion":6,"revision":3,"contentType":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"ContentType","id":"sectionHeader"}},"locale":"en-US"},"fields":{"instanceName":"PR What kind of work can be entered?","title":"What kind of work can be entered?","copy":"Campaigns that effectively shape public perception, build meaningful connections with audiences, and drive engagement through storytelling, influence, or advocacy. These can range from press and media campaigns, influencer partnerships, and social media activations to experiential events, crisis communications, and corporate social responsibility initiatives."}},{"metadata":{"tags":[],"concepts":[]},"sys":{"space":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"Space","id":"vjs6vfoxg7y6"}},"id":"2XzvJ5oB7ZLMsl3L6QeLtz","type":"Entry","createdAt":"2024-12-03T15:34:22.564Z","updatedAt":"2026-01-05T16:19:59.977Z","environment":{"sys":{"id":"master","type":"Link","linkType":"Environment"}},"publishedVersion":7,"revision":3,"contentType":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"ContentType","id":"sectionHeader"}},"locale":"en-US"},"fields":{"instanceName":"PR What are the judging criteria?","title":"What are the judging criteria?","copy":"A number of criteria will be considered during judging, weighted as follows: 20% idea; 30% PR strategy; 20% PR execution; 30% impact and results."}},{"metadata":{"tags":[],"concepts":[]},"sys":{"space":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"Space","id":"vjs6vfoxg7y6"}},"id":"3Uwqg3v0YVdomNbwaF0h35","type":"Entry","createdAt":"2026-01-05T16:20:37.340Z","updatedAt":"2026-01-05T16:20:37.340Z","environment":{"sys":{"id":"master","type":"Link","linkType":"Environment"}},"publishedVersion":3,"revision":1,"contentType":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"ContentType","id":"sectionHeader"}},"locale":"en-US"},"fields":{"instanceName":"PR Eligibility","title":"Eligibility","copy":"Work will need to have been executed during the eligibility period of 06 February 2025 - 09 April 2026."}},{"metadata":{"tags":[],"concepts":[]},"sys":{"space":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"Space","id":"vjs6vfoxg7y6"}},"id":"272Wr9NccQZFNIrv5Q4TlG","type":"Entry","createdAt":"2024-12-03T15:30:01.236Z","updatedAt":"2026-01-05T16:19:08.123Z","environment":{"sys":{"id":"master","type":"Link","linkType":"Environment"}},"publishedVersion":19,"revision":9,"contentType":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"ContentType","id":"sectionHeader"}},"locale":"en-US"},"fields":{"instanceName":"PR Is there a limit to how many times I can enter?","title":"Is there a limit to how many times I can enter?","copy":"The same piece of work can be entered up to four times in PR. \n\n__Exceptions:__\n- The same piece of work may only be entered once in ‘A. PR: Sectors’. \n- The work in Section E: Excellence in PR Craft can only be submitted and paid for by independent PR agencies or companies owned by a PR network or independent PR network. The same piece of work can be entered only once in each category within this section."}},{"metadata":{"tags":[],"concepts":[]},"sys":{"space":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"Space","id":"vjs6vfoxg7y6"}},"id":"1W4eDtXR2vDLDO00kPZtZ2","type":"Entry","createdAt":"2024-12-09T14:34:37.421Z","updatedAt":"2026-04-02T10:05:48.283Z","environment":{"sys":{"id":"master","type":"Link","linkType":"Environment"}},"publishedVersion":67,"revision":16,"contentType":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"ContentType","id":"sectionHeader"}},"locale":"en-US"},"fields":{"instanceName":"Awards / What do I need to provide with my entry?","title":"What do I need to provide with my entry?","copy":"To enter, you’ll need to complete an online entry form, which can be found on the [Cannes Lions entry site](https://awards.canneslions.com/index.cfm/entry). In the ‘Written Explanation’ section, you’ll be asked to provide the Jury with everything they need to know about your work.\n\nYou'll be required provide proof of impact, supported by verifiable evidence, this information will not be shared with the Jury, but will be used to verify claims made in your entry materials. You'll also be required to provide details of a senior executive from both the brand and the entrant company (CEO, CMO or equivalent) who can formally approve the submission. \n\nOptional supporting materials to provide additional context with your entry can also be uploaded. \n\n__Detailed information outlining the entry process can be found on our [Guide to the Cannes Lions Awards](https://www.canneslions.com/awards/awards-support/awards-entry-guide).__\n\n__More information on the rules for entry can be found on the [Summary of Key Rules for Entering](https://www.canneslions.com/awards/awards-support/rules-and-eligibility) and the handbook for our new Integrity Standards can be found on the [Integrity Standards](https://www.canneslions.com/awards/awards-support/integrity-standards).__"}},{"metadata":{"tags":[],"concepts":[]},"sys":{"space":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"Space","id":"vjs6vfoxg7y6"}},"id":"7erf9v1tOGs0gn8GD6ffTw","type":"Entry","createdAt":"2024-12-06T17:02:12.620Z","updatedAt":"2024-12-17T13:48:09.622Z","environment":{"sys":{"id":"master","type":"Link","linkType":"Environment"}},"publishedVersion":7,"revision":3,"contentType":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"ContentType","id":"textBlock"}},"locale":"en-US"},"fields":{"instanceName":"Awards / What if I don't have all the details ","heading":"What if I don’t have all the details right away?","copy":"Don’t worry – you can fill in the form in any order and save your progress at any time while it’s still in the pending stage. However, please only check out when your entry is 100% complete as no changes can be made to your entry once you’ve clicked  “Submit” and “Checkout”.","featured":true}}]}},{"metadata":{"tags":[],"concepts":[]},"sys":{"space":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"Space","id":"vjs6vfoxg7y6"}},"id":"6ZqTyzJQhceGIzxY7BiEEb","type":"Entry","createdAt":"2024-12-04T13:36:32.518Z","updatedAt":"2026-01-05T16:47:43.123Z","environment":{"sys":{"id":"master","type":"Link","linkType":"Environment"}},"publishedVersion":28,"revision":4,"contentType":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"ContentType","id":"longFormSection"}},"locale":"en-US"},"fields":{"instanceName":"PR / Entry Tips","title":"Entry Tips","copy":"![ ](//images.ctfassets.net/vjs6vfoxg7y6/2q0IyB9tvQ5FXpw83A7WEe/119fb24c650703372e6d8d3615548d80/Line_Gold.webp)","content":[{"metadata":{"tags":[],"concepts":[]},"sys":{"space":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"Space","id":"vjs6vfoxg7y6"}},"id":"5SLi8X8WUabs2tLcdfe0xD","type":"Entry","createdAt":"2024-12-03T13:33:21.469Z","updatedAt":"2026-01-05T16:17:09.572Z","environment":{"sys":{"id":"master","type":"Link","linkType":"Environment"}},"publishedVersion":11,"revision":4,"contentType":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"ContentType","id":"sectionHeader"}},"locale":"en-US"},"fields":{"instanceName":"PR What’s the Jury looking for?","title":"What’s the Jury looking for?","copy":"Strategy rooted in earned media that has real impact, beyond likes and impressions. This approach reflects and translates the nuances of customs, behaviours, and traditions that shape foundational perspectives on identity (e.g. disability, gender, race, orientation, age). Our advice is to provide as much relevant detail as possible.\n\nWhen answering the following questions keep the main element you’re trying to celebrate in mind (e.g. the storytelling, the strategy, the craft, the use of data).\n\n*The bullet points under each question are prompts designed to guide your thinking, based on the factors the jury will be considering when reviewing your work. You might not need to use them all, and that’s totally fine. They’re there to help you consider what’s most important in your work.*","content":[{"metadata":{"tags":[],"concepts":[]},"sys":{"space":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"Space","id":"vjs6vfoxg7y6"}},"id":"6QNZWn0Iq535d2jtTZDlWO","type":"Entry","createdAt":"2024-12-03T15:48:38.943Z","updatedAt":"2024-12-18T13:48:00.860Z","environment":{"sys":{"id":"master","type":"Link","linkType":"Environment"}},"publishedVersion":3,"revision":2,"contentType":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"ContentType","id":"sectionHeader"}},"locale":"en-US"},"fields":{"instanceName":"Questions","title":"Questions"}},{"metadata":{"tags":[],"concepts":[]},"sys":{"space":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"Space","id":"vjs6vfoxg7y6"}},"id":"7f2n9hgNMJGzomWRPHV5wu","type":"Entry","createdAt":"2024-12-03T15:47:46.800Z","updatedAt":"2026-01-08T16:11:18.173Z","environment":{"sys":{"id":"master","type":"Link","linkType":"Environment"}},"publishedVersion":12,"revision":4,"contentType":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"ContentType","id":"accordion"}},"locale":"en-US"},"fields":{"instanceName":"Questions","items":[{"metadata":{"tags":[],"concepts":[]},"sys":{"space":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"Space","id":"vjs6vfoxg7y6"}},"id":"DiwpkhX7rO0WAMgP5Aob3","type":"Entry","createdAt":"2024-12-03T15:38:48.253Z","updatedAt":"2024-12-18T13:48:18.736Z","environment":{"sys":{"id":"master","type":"Link","linkType":"Environment"}},"publishedVersion":6,"revision":2,"contentType":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"ContentType","id":"textBlock"}},"locale":"en-US"},"fields":{"instanceName":"1. Why is this work relevant for PR?","heading":"1. Why is this work relevant for PR?","copy":"*The word limit for this section is 100 words.*\n\n__This question is compulsory.__"}},{"metadata":{"tags":[],"concepts":[]},"sys":{"space":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"Space","id":"vjs6vfoxg7y6"}},"id":"1v7fqmCN4fH9uveNF2tcdG","type":"Entry","createdAt":"2024-12-03T15:39:18.047Z","updatedAt":"2024-12-18T13:48:32.865Z","environment":{"sys":{"id":"master","type":"Link","linkType":"Environment"}},"publishedVersion":6,"revision":2,"contentType":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"ContentType","id":"textBlock"}},"locale":"en-US"},"fields":{"instanceName":"2. Brand Context","heading":"2. Brand Context","copy":"Please provide any brand and cultural context that would help the jury understand any cultural, national or regional nuances applicable to this work.\n\nYou should outline why this piece of work was created for this particular brand, in this market, at this particular moment in time, with all of the cultural and market conditions.\n\n*The word limit for this section is 250 words.*\n\n__This question is compulsory.__\n"}},{"metadata":{"tags":[],"concepts":[]},"sys":{"space":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"Space","id":"vjs6vfoxg7y6"}},"id":"65UVK8nrQ6lvZMbyiTLBY1","type":"Entry","createdAt":"2024-12-03T15:40:03.189Z","updatedAt":"2025-01-15T15:42:00.215Z","environment":{"sys":{"id":"master","type":"Link","linkType":"Environment"}},"publishedVersion":10,"revision":3,"contentType":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"ContentType","id":"textBlock"}},"locale":"en-US"},"fields":{"instanceName":"3. Objective(s)","heading":"3. Objective(s)","copy":"Please select the objective(s) that were most important when creating this work. What the work set out to achieve.\n\nFor the jury to accurately assess the work, they must first establish its objectives. As you progress through the entry system, you must link your results back to each objective selected.\n\n- Overachieve on brand metrics (e.g. awareness, engagement)\n- Change consumer behaviour (e.g. attitudes, frequency, penetration)\n- Create short-term sales growth (e.g. sales, promotions, discounts)\n- Improve brand health (e.g. consideration, preference)\n- Create sustained sales success (e.g. profit, sales)\n- Create long-term brand and sales growth (e.g. shareholder value, retention, loyalty)\n\n*There is no word limit for this section.*\n\n__This question is compulsory.__"}},{"metadata":{"tags":[],"concepts":[]},"sys":{"space":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"Space","id":"vjs6vfoxg7y6"}},"id":"5JaThLViV75OlYO5qMkUm3","type":"Entry","createdAt":"2024-12-03T15:41:18.693Z","updatedAt":"2024-12-18T13:48:57.922Z","environment":{"sys":{"id":"master","type":"Link","linkType":"Environment"}},"publishedVersion":8,"revision":2,"contentType":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"ContentType","id":"textBlock"}},"locale":"en-US"},"fields":{"instanceName":"4. Summary of the work","heading":"4. Summary of the work","copy":"Please provide a summary of your entry.\n- Describe the background and context behind the work?\n- What creative challenges did the work face?\n\nThis should be an overview of the work and an analysis of its effectiveness. You should describe how the objectives of the campaign have been successfully met.\n\n*The word limit for this section is 150 words.*\n\n__This question is compulsory.__\n"}},{"metadata":{"tags":[],"concepts":[]},"sys":{"space":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"Space","id":"vjs6vfoxg7y6"}},"id":"4r00Oz0Fi6uj05xXtUYRIR","type":"Entry","createdAt":"2024-12-03T15:42:07.325Z","updatedAt":"2024-12-18T13:49:13.296Z","environment":{"sys":{"id":"master","type":"Link","linkType":"Environment"}},"publishedVersion":7,"revision":2,"contentType":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"ContentType","id":"textBlock"}},"locale":"en-US"},"fields":{"instanceName":"5. Describe the creative idea (20% of vote)","heading":"5. Describe the creative idea (20% of vote)","copy":"Consider the following when describing the creative idea:\n\n- Does the work cross boundaries/have the ability to think beyond conventional boundaries or categories?\n- Does the work evoke surprise/have the power to continue captivating attention?\n- Does the work transcend the original challenge?\n- Does the work incorporate breakthrough thinking?\n- Demonstrate how the idea was designed to earn attention from the target audience?\n\n*The word limit for this section is 150 words.*\n\n__This question is compulsory.__\n"}},{"metadata":{"tags":[],"concepts":[]},"sys":{"space":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"Space","id":"vjs6vfoxg7y6"}},"id":"2raqR7RNwdQOhJbDGG4b4I","type":"Entry","createdAt":"2024-12-03T15:42:56.437Z","updatedAt":"2024-12-18T13:49:23.511Z","environment":{"sys":{"id":"master","type":"Link","linkType":"Environment"}},"publishedVersion":7,"revision":2,"contentType":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"ContentType","id":"textBlock"}},"locale":"en-US"},"fields":{"instanceName":"6. Describe the PR strategy (30% of vote)","heading":"6. Describe the PR strategy (30% of vote)","copy":"Consider the following when describing the execution:\n\n- How effectively was the concept brought to life?\n- What specific PR implementation strategies were employed to ensure success?\n- Which channels were utilised for placement, and were they optimal for reaching the target audience?\n- How extensive was the scale of the execution in relation to the defined objectives?\n- Did the execution adapt to any unexpected challenges or changes in the environment?\n\n*The word limit for this section is 150 words.*\n\n__This question is compulsory.__\n"}},{"metadata":{"tags":[],"concepts":[]},"sys":{"space":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"Space","id":"vjs6vfoxg7y6"}},"id":"98MyMehph0MjqWhmfg9Sd","type":"Entry","createdAt":"2024-12-03T15:43:36.125Z","updatedAt":"2024-12-18T13:49:32.568Z","environment":{"sys":{"id":"master","type":"Link","linkType":"Environment"}},"publishedVersion":6,"revision":2,"contentType":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"ContentType","id":"textBlock"}},"locale":"en-US"},"fields":{"instanceName":"7. Describe the PR execution (20% of vote)","heading":"7. Describe the PR execution (20% of vote)","copy":"Consider the following when describing the execution:\n\n- How effectively was the concept brought to life?\n- What specific PR implementation strategies were employed to ensure success?\n- Which channels were utilised for placement, and were they optimal for reaching the target audience?\n- How extensive was the scale of the execution in relation to the defined objectives?\n- Did the execution adapt to any unexpected challenges or changes in the environment?\n\n*The word limit for this section is 150 words.*\n\n__This question is compulsory.__\n"}},{"metadata":{"tags":[],"concepts":[]},"sys":{"space":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"Space","id":"vjs6vfoxg7y6"}},"id":"2qLSc66eshqkSOSJTdzEC3","type":"Entry","createdAt":"2024-12-03T15:44:21.460Z","updatedAt":"2024-12-18T13:49:40.685Z","environment":{"sys":{"id":"master","type":"Link","linkType":"Environment"}},"publishedVersion":8,"revision":2,"contentType":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"ContentType","id":"textBlock"}},"locale":"en-US"},"fields":{"instanceName":"8. List the impact and results (30% of vote)","heading":"8. List the impact and results (30% of vote)","copy":"Please refer back to the objectives selected when answering this question. Consider:\n\n- performance against brand metrics (e.g. awareness, engagement)\n- change in consumer behaviour (e.g. attitudes, frequency, penetration)\n- indications of short-, medium- or long-term sales success (e.g. shareholder value, profit, revenue, ROI, sales, share)\n- brand health improvement (e.g. consideration, preference)\n- potential changes in government regulations or societal/economic events (e.g. changes in economic, political, social factors).\n\n*The word limit for this section is 250 words.*\n\n__This question is compulsory.__\n"}},{"metadata":{"tags":[],"concepts":[]},"sys":{"space":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"Space","id":"vjs6vfoxg7y6"}},"id":"3ok2UXmMMMBJVhsEDcdEvk","type":"Entry","createdAt":"2025-01-13T10:09:00.432Z","updatedAt":"2026-01-21T10:22:33.527Z","environment":{"sys":{"id":"master","type":"Link","linkType":"Environment"}},"publishedVersion":27,"revision":9,"contentType":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"ContentType","id":"textBlock"}},"locale":"en-US"},"fields":{"instanceName":"Was Artificial Intelligence (AI) used in the work or the entry materials? ","heading":"Was Artificial Intelligence (AI) used in the work or the entry materials? ","copy":"If yes, you must disclose how it was used, considering the following:\n- What AI tools were used?\n- What was the extent of AI use in the final work?\n- What were the data sources or inputs for any AI-generated elements, and where did they originate?\n\nFor guidance on acceptable AI usage please refer to the [LIONS Integrity Handbook](https://assets.ctfassets.net/vjs6vfoxg7y6/6n1777aqrVS3u2UbbG0XC6/9207dc5d36fcdf2a8d5061560a8919bd/LIONS_Integrity_Handbook_12.11.2025.pdf).\nMisrepresentation or wilful omission may result in your entry being withdrawn. \n\n*The word limit for this section is 150 words.*\n\n__This question is compulsory.__"}},{"metadata":{"tags":[],"concepts":[]},"sys":{"space":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"Space","id":"vjs6vfoxg7y6"}},"id":"5i76ZmDq8008Pju8Spj974","type":"Entry","createdAt":"2025-01-13T10:09:22.278Z","updatedAt":"2026-01-21T11:01:23.067Z","environment":{"sys":{"id":"master","type":"Link","linkType":"Environment"}},"publishedVersion":18,"revision":6,"contentType":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"ContentType","id":"textBlock"}},"locale":"en-US"},"fields":{"instanceName":"Sustainability Context","heading":"Sustainability Context","copy":"As a member of AdNet Zero, we believe in the collective responsibility to reduce the carbon impact of developing, producing and running advertising to achieve real net zero. \nPlease outline how this piece of work aligns with the brand's sustainability strategy and consider the following: \n\n- Environmental cost: What steps were taken to measure, reduce and manage the environmental footprint of the work across its life cycle? \n- Meaningful claims: What evidence can you provide to support any sustainability related claims made in the work, ensuring they are credible and meaningful? \n- Long-term commitments: How do the sustainability-related claims reflect the brand's broader strategy and focus on relevant issues? \n\nPlease provide details of the benchmarks used to measure the success of this work's contribution to the overall strategy. This could include but is not limited to targets related to advertising business operations - advertising production - media planning and buying. \n\n*There is no word limit for this section.*\n\n__This question is optional.__"}},{"metadata":{"tags":[],"concepts":[]},"sys":{"space":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"Space","id":"vjs6vfoxg7y6"}},"id":"1fb8wjY1tbKn2vLUg6rj2x","type":"Entry","createdAt":"2025-01-13T10:10:17.740Z","updatedAt":"2026-01-21T11:01:09.849Z","environment":{"sys":{"id":"master","type":"Link","linkType":"Environment"}},"publishedVersion":16,"revision":6,"contentType":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"ContentType","id":"textBlock"}},"locale":"en-US"},"fields":{"instanceName":"Equity, Representation and Accessibility (if appropriate) ","heading":"Equity, Representation and Accessibility (if appropriate) ","copy":"Based on the brief, what consideration was given to representation and diversity of the team(s) involved? \n\nWhat consideration was given to accessibility in the work and what role did DEI thinking play throughout the development of the work? \n\n*There is no word limit for this section.*\n\n__This question is optional.__"}},{"metadata":{"tags":[],"concepts":[]},"sys":{"space":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"Space","id":"vjs6vfoxg7y6"}},"id":"5q7jBuUJDnYIbByB3NtSWZ","type":"Entry","createdAt":"2024-12-18T14:17:59.388Z","updatedAt":"2026-01-06T16:09:38.802Z","environment":{"sys":{"id":"master","type":"Link","linkType":"Environment"}},"publishedVersion":16,"revision":5,"contentType":{"sys":{"type":"Link","linkType":"ContentType","id":"textBlock"}},"locale":"en-US"},"fields":{"instanceName":"Confidential
